Bisection feasibility details:
```
def feasible(T, a_nd, c_nd, diag_base, c_diag, h0):
    xlo=0; xhi=1
    pos = a_nd > eps; neg = a_nd < -eps
    if any(pos): xhi = min(xhi, h0 + min((T - c_nd[pos]) / a_nd[pos]))? Wait affine: c + a*(x-h0) <= T -> if a>0: x <= h0 + (T-c)/a. If a<0: x >= h0 + (T-c)/a (since divide negative flips? Formula x-h0 <= (T-c)/a for a>0; for a<0, x-h0 >= (T-c)/a. So h0 + (T-c)/a works as lower? For negative, (T-c)/a is maybe negative; x >= that. So xlo = max over neg of h0+(T-c)/a; xhi=min over pos same.

def coord_solve(c, h, p, n, cmax):
    h0=h[p]
    if h0 < 1e-14 or h0 > 1-1e-14: maybe still update? If at boundary, affine only and diag maybe. We can solve; but for speed skip if unaff_max>=cmax? If skip boundary could miss improvement. But if h at 0/1, moving may reduce. Include.
    m_lo=max(0,n-1-p); m_hi=min(2*n-2,p+n-1)
    ms=np.arange(m_lo,m_hi+1)
    s=ms-(n-1)
    jf=p+s; jb=p-s
    vf=(jf>=0)&(jf<n); vb=(jb>=0)&(jb<n)
    a=np.zeros(len(ms));
    if np.any(vf): a[vf]+=1-h[jf[vf]]
    if np.any(vb): a[vb]-=h[jb[vb]]
    diag_idx=m_center-m_lo (n-1-m_lo)
    c_diag=c[n-1]
    a[diag_idx]=0
    c_aff=c[m_lo:m_hi+1]
    non_diag = np.ones(len(ms),bool); non_diag[diag_idx]=False
    a_nd=a[non_diag]; c_nd=c_aff[non_diag]
    pos=a_nd>1e-14; neg=a_nd<-1e-14
    # masks already
    Llo=0? We can set lower bound max(0, maybe cmin). Use unaff_max as lower? The minimal T cannot be < unaff_max because unaffected lags. So Llo=unaff_max. But T can be lower than current max.     Lhi=cmax

    for 36:

       if feasible: Lhi=T else Llo=T
    T=Lhi + 1e-12 maybe.
    # find x feasible
    xlo=0; xhi=1
    if pos any: xhi=min(xhi, np.min(h0 + (T - c_nd[pos])/a_nd[pos]))
    if neg any: xlo=max(xlo, np.max(h0 + (T - c_nd[neg])/a_nd[neg]))
    # clip
    # diag
    Cq=T - c_diag + h0 - h0*h0
    if Cq < -1e-12: return None? feasible should not
    elif Cq < 0.25 - 1e-15:
       D=1-4Cq; if D<0 no; r1...;

    if no cand return h0

Let's design `project_feasible` robust and fast:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    S=n/2
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    for _ in range(100):
       d=S-h.sum()
       if abs(d)<1e-14: break
       if d>0:
          room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
          if tot<1e-15: break
          # add proportion to room but could clip; use iterative waterfill better
          h=np.clip(h + d/tot*room,0,1)
       else:
          tot=h.sum()
          if tot<1e-15: break
          h=np.clip(h + d/tot*h,0,1)
    # exact adjust: after loop, small diff in a free variable
    diff=S-h.sum()
    # find index with room depending sign; adjust one by one if possible
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       if diff>0:
          idx=np.where(h<1-1e-15)[0]
          if len(idx):
             step=min(abs(diff),1-h[idx[0]])
             h[idx[0]]+=step
             # if still diff loop?
       else: similar
    # final rescale? Could use waterfill exact with binary.
```

`coord_descent_inplace(h, n, max_passes, deadline, rng, random_order=True, tol=1e-12)`:
- h=project_feasible(h.copy(),n)
- c=correlate(h,1-h)
- cmax=max(c)
- passes=0
- for pass in range(max_passes):
   if time>deadline or cmax/dx < best? We'll return.
   order = rng.permutation(n) if random else np.arange(n)
   improved=False
   # compute prefix/suffix once at start and after updates? We'll update after accepted.
   pref,suff = prefix_suffix(c)
   for p in order:
      h0=h[p]
      m_lo... m_hi
      # unaff_max using pref/suff. Need if pref/suff up to date? We'll recompute after each accept? Could be costly but okay. Then unaff exact.
      cmax_current = c.max()? We can keep cmax variable. After accept set. After skip same.
      if cmax_current <= unaff_max + 1e-13: # cannot lower global max
         continue
      # Bisection: lower=unaff_max (maybe if Llo>=cmax skip), upper=cmax_current
      # If h0 fixed? We can still solve. 
      x=coord_solve(c, h, p, n, cmax_current, unaff_max, low_mask maybe)
      if x is not None and abs(x-h0)>1e-15:
         # evaluate newmax for x
         # compute new values for affected slice and max
         newaff=c_aff + a*(x-h0), set diag
         newmax=max(unaff_max, newaff.max())
         if newmax < cmax_current - 1e-13:
            # accept
            h[p]=x
            c[m_lo:m_hi+1]=newaff
            cmax_current=newmax
            # update pref/suff: recompute O(n)
            pref,suff, cmax_val = prefix_suffix(c) # maybe cmax newmax?
            if cmax_val < cmax_current: cmax_current=cmax_val
            improved=True
   end for
   # end pass; if no improved break; else recompute c from h to remove drift? Could occasionally.
   if not improved: break
   # Every 10 passes recompute c=correlate to correct numerical, cmax
   if pass%10==9:
      c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cmax=c.max(); pref,suff=prefix_suffix(c); if cmax<best? 
- return h, cmax*dx

Need `coord_solve` needs c_aff etc. It will allocate a array each call. To speed, we can compute a array only for p where possible. Bisection uses masks fixed.

Implement `coord_solve` top-level with parameters `c, h, p, n, cmax, unaff_max, iters=24`.
Pseudo more concretely:
```
def coord_solve(c,h,p,n,cmax,unaff_max,iters=24):
    h0=h[p]
    mlo=max(0,n-1-p); mhi=min(2*n-2,p+n-1)
    diag=mlo + (n-1-mlo)
    ms = np.arange(mlo,mhi+1)
    s = ms-(n-1)
    jf = p+s; jb=p-s
    vf = (jf>=0)&(jf<n); vb=(jb>=0)&(jb<n)
    a=np.zeros(len(ms), dtype=float)
    if np.any(vf): a[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
    if np.any(vb): a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
    a[diag]=0.0
    c_aff = c[mlo:mhi+1]
    cdiag = c[n-1]
    nd = np.ones(len(ms), bool); nd[diag]=False
    a_nd=a[nd]; c_nd=c_aff[nd]
    eps=1e-14
    pos = a_nd > eps; neg = a_nd < -eps
    maxcnt=pos.sum()+neg.sum()
    lo=unaff_max
    hi=cmax
    if hi <= lo + 1e-12: return None
    # If even at hi infeasible due numeric? x=h0 feasible. Add margin to hi.
    # Bisection:
    for _ in range(iters):
       T=(lo+hi)*0.5
       xlo=0.0; xhi=1.0
       if np.any(pos):
          b = h0 + (T - c_nd[pos]) / a_nd[pos]
          v = b.min()
          if v < xhi: xhi = v
       if np.any(neg):
          b = h0 + (T - c_nd[neg]) / a_nd[neg]
          v = b.max()
          if v > xlo: xlo = v
       if xlo > xhi + 1e-14: feasible=False
       else:
          # clip xlo/xhi to 0/1? For diag, if xlo>1 or xhi<0 infeasible
          xlo_c = max(0.0, min(1.0, xlo))
          xhi_c = min(1.0, max(0.0, xhi))
          if xlo_c > xhi_c + 1e-14: feasible=False
          else:
             Cq = T - cdiag + h0 - h0*h0
             if Cq < -1e-13: feasible=False
             elif Cq >= 0.25 - 1e-14: feasible=True
             else:
                D = 1.0 - 4.0*Cq
                if D < 0: feasible=False
                else:
                   r1=(1-√D)/2; r2=(1+√D)/2
                   # interval [xlo_c,xhi_c] intersects [0,r1]? if xlo_c <= r1 (and xhi_c>=0) yes
                   if xlo_c <= r1 + 1e-13 or xhi_c >= r2 - 1e-13:
                      feasible=True
                   else: feasible=False
       if feasible: hi=T
       else: lo=T
    T=hi
    # candidate x
    candidates=[]
    # compute xlo/xhi for T maybe add eps
    xe= T + max(1e-12, 1e-10*abs(T))  # small margin? If T too low due numeric, infeasible.
    ... compute xlo,xhi,candidates
    # Evaluate cand predicted max for a few cand
    best_x=h0; best_m = cmax+1
    for x in cand:
       # affine max
       vals = c_nd + a_nd*(x-h0)
       affmax=vals.max() if len else 0
       diagv=cdiag + x - x*x - h0 + h0*h0
       m=max(unaff_max, affmax, diagv)
       if m < best_m: best_m=m; best_x=x
    return best_x, best_m # maybe
```

Prefix/suffix recompute after accept: top-level `prefix_suffix(c):` returns pref length n+1? We need pref[i]=max c[0:i] (first i elements), suff[i]=max c[i:] maybe with -inf. For unaff outside [mlo,mhi]: max(c[:mlo], c[mhi+1:]). Use:
```
def prefix_suffix(c):
  n=len(c)
  pref=np.zeros(n+1); pref[0]=-np.inf
  for i in range(n): pref[i+1]=max(pref[i],c[i])
  suff=np.zeros(n+1); suff[n]=-np.inf
  for i in reversed(range(n)): suff[i]=max(suff[i+1],c[i])
  return pref,suff
```
O(n) Python loop 1400 per accept, okay. Could use np.maximum.accumulate for speed:
```
pref=np.maximum.accumulate(np.concatenate([[-np.inf],c]))
suff=np.maximum.accumulate(np.concatenate([c,[-np.inf]])[::-1])[::-1]
```
This allocates but fine. Maybe simple loops? Use numpy accumulate:
- `pref = np.empty(n+1); pref[0]=-inf; pref[1:]=np.maximum.accumulate(c)`
- `suff = np.empty(n+1); suff[n]=-inf; suff[:n]=np.maximum.accumulate(c[::-1])[::-1]`
Then outside max = max(pref[mlo], suff[mhi+1]) where suff index start included. Good.

best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
for it in range(max_iter):
  if time>deadline break
  c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
  cmax = c.max()
  # weights softmax alpha*(c - cmax)/scale? scale maybe cmax*10? Let z = alpha_extra*(c - cmax), max 0 => exp >=.
  w = np.exp(alpha_para*(c - cmax)); w/=w.sum()
  grad = zeros(n)
  for s in -(n-1)..n-1:
     wi = w[(n-1)+s]*dx? We can include dx later. If using normalized objective, weights scaled by dx.
     if wi < 1e-8: continue
     if s>=0:
        grad[:n-s] += wi*(1-h[s:])
        if s>0: grad[s:] -= wi*h[:n-s]
     else:
        t=-s
        grad[t:] += wi*(1-h[:n-t])
        if t >0: grad[:n-t] -= wi*h[t:]
  # derivative of smooth objective (1/dx? Actually c normalized by dx. weights sum to1; smooth max normalized = dx*(cmax + logsumexp/alpha). derivative = dx * sum w_i grad c_i.
  grad *= dx

  g=grad - grad.mean()

  base_obj = dx*(cmax + logsumexp(alpha_param*(c-cmax))/alpha) # but cmax used; approximate

     hp=project_feasible(h - step*g, n)

Low-dimensional optimization implementation:
```
def sine_odd_from_coefs(coefs, n):
    x = (np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
    theta = 0.5*np.pi*x
    h = np.zeros(n)
    for m,c in enumerate(coefs):
        if abs(c)>1e-12: h += c*np.sin((2*m+1)*theta)
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```
But raw may be negative/outside range; project clips. For Nelder-Mead, objective can be weird if coeff large. We'll restrict coefficient starts small and initial within. def legal_sine_obj(coefs, n, terms):

Now shape search implementation details.

`make_shape(kind,n)`:
Use cell centers: `x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n`.
- 'sin1': `h = (np.pi/4)*np.sin(0.5*np.pi*x)` (same as sinθ). Project.
- 'pow_p': `h0=np.sin(0.5*np.pi*x)**p`; project by scaling? To satisfy area, we can compute `h = h0 / (h0.mean())`? Because dx cancels: sum target n/2; if h0 mean m, h = h0/m. But bounds may exceed 1. For p<1, sin^p has average maybe; max1; scaling could exceed. Use project. Or pre-normalize: `h = h0 / (h0.mean())`, then clip/project. Good.
 p values: 0.7,1.0,1.3,1.6 maybe.
- 'tri': `h=1.0 - np.abs(x-1.0)`; area1? continuous area1, discrete approx; project.
- 'sin3': `base=np.sin(0.5πx); h=base + b*np.sin(1.5πx)`? Since θ=0.5πx, sin3θ. Use b = a1*ratio. Need area. We'll project; raw could negative if ratio too high. Choose ratio small [-0.2, -0.1, 0.1,0.2]. Scale to area: before project, multiply by target_sum/sum? `s = n/2 / raw.sum()` if sum>0; raw=s*raw; project. This ensures mean. If raw out of bounds, project clips.

```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target=0.5*n
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    # If all same? waterfill.
    lo=-2.0; hi=2.0
    # ensure h within maybe? We can first clip? The projection of unclipped onto [0,1] sum target. If h outside far, binary t: x=clip(h-t,0,1).
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       s=np.sum(np.clip(h-mid,0,1))
       if s > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    x=np.clip(h-(lo+hi)*0.5,0,1)
    # fix small diff
    diff=target-x.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       # adjust free elements
       if diff>0:
          idx=np.argsort(1-x)??? For nearest, choose elements with most room. But simple loop:
          for i in range(n):
             step=min(diff,1-x[i]); if step>0: x[i]+=step; diff-=step; if abs(diff)<1e-15 break
       else:
          for i in range(n):
             step=min(-diff,x[i]); if step>0: x[i]-=step; diff+=step; if abs(diff)<1e-15 break
    return x
```
After waterfill, diff should be tiny. Loop O(n) worst. Good.
Could use 60 iterations enough; target within [0,n], h perhaps values [0,1] after shape, t range [-1,1] enough but use [-2,2]. If h is all 0 and target>0, sum x at t mid? If h=0, clip(-mid,0,1). For mid negative, x=1; can hit. lo=-2 hi=2 okay. 80 gives precision 1e-3? 4/2^80 tiny.

But if we call project many times, 80*700=56k ops, okay. Could reduce to 50.

Pseudo code:
```
def coord_solve(c, h, p, n, cmax, unaff_max, iters, entry):
    mlo,mhi,diag_rel,frel,fcidx,srel,scidx = entry
    h0 = h[p]
    length = mhi-mlo+1
    a = np.zeros(length, dtype=float)
    if frel.size:
        a[frel] += 1.0 - h[fcidx]
    if srel.size:
        a[srel] -= h[scidx]
    a[diag_rel] = 0.0
    c_aff = c[mlo:mhi+1]
    cdiag = c[n-1]
    # build nd arrays
    if diag_rel == 0:
       a_nd = a[1:]; c_nd = c_aff[1:]
    elif diag_rel == length-1:
       a_nd = a[:-1]; c_nd = c_aff[:-1]
    else:
       a_nd = np.concatenate((a[:diag_rel], a[diag_rel+1:]))
       c_nd = np.concatenate((c_aff[:diag_rel], c_aff[diag_rel+1:]))
    # if no affine constraints, handle just diag? We can still.
    pos = a_nd > 1e-14
    neg = a_nd < -1e-14
    if np.any(pos):
       ap = a_nd[pos]; cp = c_nd[pos]
       inv_ap = 1.0/ap; const_ap = h0 - cp/ap
       has_pos=True
    else: has_pos=False
    ... neg
    lo = max(0.0, unaff_max)
    hi = cmax
    if hi <= lo + 1e-13:
       return None
    # ensure hi feasible? maybe if hi<0? no.
    # bisection
    for _ in range(iters):
       T=0.5*(lo+hi)
       if has_pos:
          xhi = float(np.min(T*inv_ap + const_ap))
          if xhi > 1.0: xhi=1.0
       else: xhi=1.0
       if has_neg:
          xlo = float(np.max(T*inv_an + const_an))
          if xlo < 0.0: xlo=0.0
       else: xlo=0.0
       feasible=False
       if xlo <= 1.0 and xhi >= 0.0:
          L=min(max(xlo,0.0),1.0); R=max(xhi,0.0)? Wait if xhi<0 none; if xlo>1 none.
          L = max(0.0, min(xlo,1.0))? If xlo>1 no feasible. Let's do:
          if xhi < -1e-15 or xlo > 1+1e-15: infeas
          else L=max(0,xlo); R=min(1,xhi); if L<=R: check diag
       Cq = T - cdiag + h0 - h0*h0
       if Cq < -1e-12: infeas
       elif Cq >= 0.25 - 1e-14: feasible=True
       else:
          D=1-4Cq
          if D<0: infeas
          else:
             r1=0.5*(1-sqrt(D)); r2=0.5*(1+sqrt(D))
             if L <= r1 + 1e-14 or R >= r2 - 1e-14: feasible=True
       if feasible: hi=T else lo=T
    # Candidate at T = hi + margin
    T = hi + max(1e-12, 1e-10*abs(hi))
    # compute aff interval with same
    ... if infeasible return None? To be safe use hi + larger or return h0.
    # create intervals
    intervals=[]
    if aff valid:
       if Cq...:
           if Cq>=.25: add [L,R]
           else: add [L,min(R,r1)], [max(L,r2),R] if overlap
    if no intervals: return None
    cand list: for each interval add L,R,(L+R)/2, maybe 0.5 if within, h0 if within.
    # unique
    cand=np.array(cands)
    cand=cand[(cand>=0)&(cand<=1)]
    if empty return None
    cand.sort()
    # approximate unique
    uniq=[]; last=None; for v in cand: if last None or v-last>1e-10: uniq.append(v); last=v
    cand=np.array(uniq)
    # evaluate
    aff_vals = c_nd[None,:] + a_nd[None,:]*(cand[:,None]-h0)
    if aff_vals.size:
       max_aff=aff_vals.max(axis=1); sum_aff=aff_vals.sum(axis=1)
    else:
       max_aff=np.zeros(len(cand)); sum_aff=np.zeros(len(cand))
    diag_vals = cdiag + cand - cand*cand - h0 + h0*h0
    max_vals = np.maximum(unaff_max, np.maximum(max_aff, diag_vals))
    scores = max_vals + 1e-8 * (sum_aff + diag_vals)
    idx = int(np.argmin(np.where(max_vals < cmax + 1e-11, scores, max_vals + 1e6)))? We need prioritize max lower, then score. Use lexsort: primary max_vals, secondary scores.
    order = np.lexsort((scores, max_vals)); idx=order[0]
    x=float(cand[idx]); newmax=float(max_vals[idx]); score=float(scores[idx])
    new_aff = c_aff + a*(x-h0)
    new_aff[diag_rel] = cdiag + x*x? x - x*x - h0 + h0*h0
    # recompute newmax exact? newmax_val = max(unaff_max, new_aff.max())
    newmax = float(max(unaff_max, new_aff.max()))
    score = newmax + 1e-8 * float(new_aff.sum())
    return x,new_aff,newmax,score
```

Now `coord_descent` implementation with maps and time:
```
def coord_descent(h,n,deadline,rng,iters,max_passes,maps,plateau=8,strict_tol=1e-11,tie_tol=1e-10):
   h=project_feasible(h.copy(),n)
   c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cmax=float(c.max()); csum=float(c.sum())
   best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cmax
   cmax_cur=cmax; csum_cur=csum
   refresh pref/suff
   plateau=0
   for pass in range(max_passes):
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      order = rng.permutation(n) if rng is not None else np.arange(n)
      pass_improved=False; pass_strict=False
      for p in order:
         if time.time()>deadline: break
         entry=maps[p]
         mlo,mhi=entry[0],entry[1]
         umax=max(pref[mlo],suff[mhi+1])
         if not np.isfinite(umax): umax=0.0
         if cmax_cur <= umax + 1e-13: continue
         res=coord_solve(... cmax=cmax_cur, unaff_max=umax, iters=iters, entry=entry)
         if res is None: continue
         x,new_aff,newmax,score=res
         old_slice = c[mlo:mhi+1] # for csum update maybe we can compute oldsum? We'll recompute csum after accept by c.sum? To avoid, do csum_cur = csum_cur + new_aff.sum() - old_slice.sum(). old_slice sum O(len) each accepted only. Fine.
         if newmax < cmax_cur - strict_tol or (newmax <= cmax_cur + tie_tol and newmax <= best_c + tie_tol and score < cmax_cur + 1e-9*csum_cur - 1e-11):
             # accept
             h[p]=x
             delta = new_aff.sum() - old_slice.sum()
             c[mlo:mhi+1]=new_aff
             csum_cur += delta
             cmax_cur = float(max(umax, newmax)) # but due other? exact newmax was max; use cmax_cur=newmax maybe; recompute safe? newmax includes umax exact. Set cmax_cur=newmax.
             # for prefix/suff refresh
             cmax_pref,pref,suff = refresh(c)
             # cmax_pref should equal cmax_cur; use cmax_pref for accuracy
             cmax_cur = cmax_pref
             csum_cur = float(c.sum())? We updated but refresh csum? We can compute csum_cur = csum_cur (avoid c.sum). But due old_slice sum maybe exact; okay.
             if cmax_cur < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c=cmax_cur; best_h=h.copy()
             pass_improved=True
             if newmax < cmax_old - strict_tol: pass_strict=True
         end if
      end for
      if not pass_improved: break
      if not pass_strict: plateau+=1
      else: plateau=0
      if plateau > plateau_limit: break
   end for
   # final project?
   h=project_feasible(h,n)
   c_final=compute? use correlate max
   cmaxf=c.max()
   if cmaxf < best_c: best_c=cmaxf; best_h=h.copy()
   return best_h,best_c
```
Need `old_slice` if accept. We only need if accepted; create before? We can in accept do `oldsum = c[mlo:mhi+1].sum()` but c still old. Fine.
`cmax_old` store before accept to check strict.
Potential issue: After accept, `cmax_cur = cmax_pref` could be greater than `newmax` due numerical outside? Use cmax_pref. Tie condition for next uses updated.
`csum_cur` recompute after accept? `csum_cur += new_aff.sum() - oldsum`. To avoid oldsum calculation cost, maybe skip csum and only use score primary max? For tie score, need current. We can maintain approximate with recompute after accept: `csum_cur = csum_cur + float(new_aff.sum()) - float(old_slice.sum())`. old_slice from c before update. That's one sum O(len) only accepts. Good.
But in tie score before accept, current `csum_cur` maintained. Good.

Let's refine `make_shape` for candidates:
```
def make_shape(kind, n):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
    th=0.5*np.pi*x
    if kind==0: h=(np.pi/4.0)*np.sin(th)
    elif kind==1:
       h=np.sin(th); h=np.maximum(h,0)**1.0? `np.sin(th)` nonnegative on [0,π]. For p: h=np.power(np.maximum(np.sin(th),0), p)

def build_maps(n):
 maps=[]
 for p in range(n):
   mlo = min(p, n-1-p)
   mhi = max(p+n-1, 2*n-2-p)
   f_lo = max(mlo, n-1-p)
   f_hi = min(mhi, 2*n-2-p)
   if f_lo <= f_hi:
      nlen=f_hi-f_lo+1
      rel_f = np.arange(f_lo-mlo, f_hi-mlo+1, dtype=int)
      jf = p + f_lo - (n-1) + np.arange(nlen)  # since m increases
   m_hi etc
   s_lo=max(mlo,p); s_hi=min(mhi,p+n-1)
   rel_s...
   jb = p + (n-1) - s_lo - np.arange(nlen) # m increases, jb decreases
   diag_rel=n-1-mlo
   maps.append((mlo,mhi,diag_rel,rel_f,jf,rel_s,jb))
 return maps
```

Now implement `refresh_c(c)` using c opt.

Let's think about sum constraint in projection and coordinate updates. Coordinate update does not preserve sum! Changing h[p] by x-h0 alters sum. The objective constraints require sum fixed. Our CD as written allows changing one variable without adjusting others; after a pass or each update, we need project to feasible. Previous code projected after full pass. In in-place CD, h sum drifts. We must handle feasibility during coordinate updates. Important!

The original coordinate descent updated one variable then at end of pass did `project_feasible`. But exact c update assumed only h[p] changed; after pass project changes all variables, c recomputed. That's okay. In our in-place CD, if we update many variables without projecting, sum violates constraint; objective evaluation during pass not valid for constraint. We need enforce sum after each update or at least keep within threshold.

We can adapt: After accept a coordinate update, adjust another variable to keep sum? Or project after each pass and recompute c. The previous approach: h changes throughout pass (sum drifts), c updated based on changes, but final pass projection restores. However the objective C during pass under infeasible h not meaningful; but coordinate solver确保了 reducing c of infeasible vector? The constraints include sum; ignoring during pass may still produce feasible final with lower C? It worked perhaps. But our acceptance criteria based on c of infeasible h could be misleading. We need manage.

Options:
1. Update h[p] and immediately project the whole h to feasibility (waterfill) after each update. Then recompute c and prefix/suffix. This is costly: projection O(n log), recount correlate O(n^2) per update. Too heavy.
2. Use a paired update: when changing h[p] by δ, adjust another variable h[q] by -δ/2 or -δ to preserve sum. But projection bounds. Could choose q not affecting active lags? Hard. Or adjust all variables proportionally with waterfill? 
3. Work in equality-constrained coordinate space: For a given p, choose x for h[p] and adjust one other variable r (e.g., p+1 mod n) to satisfy sum, while respecting bounds. This 2D update complex.
4. Allow drift within pass but project at pass end; ensure pass only accepted if final C lower. This is what previous did. We can accept coordinate moves based on infeasible c; not exact but heuristic. To be safer, after pass projection, if final C lower than best keep. If not, rollback. But within pass, c updates not valid after projection. We can still use as search. The objective improvement may be found after projection. Need track best h at feasible checkpoints.

Better: Do block coordinate: Within a pass, we can update h variables and c as if only those changed (sum-free), then at end project and recompute C. If lower, accept pass; else rollback to pass start. This is similar previous. We can do this in a `coord_descent_pass`:
- h_start, c_start, cmax_start, best tracking.
- For p in order: use current working c/h (infeasible) to find x and update c/h.

Let's write `coord_descent` with pass rollback:
```
def coord_descent(h,n,deadline,rng,iters,max_passes,maps,plateau=8):
  h_feas=project...
  c_feas=opt_corr; cmax_feas=max; best_h=h_feas.copy(); best_c=cmax_feas
  csum_feas=c_feas.sum()
  pass_count=0; plateau=0
  for pass in range(max_passes):
    if time>deadline: break
    h_work=h_feas.copy(); c_work=c_feas.copy(); cmax_work=cmax_feas; csum_work=csum_feas
    refresh pref_work,suff_work
    improved_work=False; strict_work=False
    order=...
    for p in order:
       if time>deadline: break
       entry=maps[p]
       umax=max(...); if not finite 0
       if cmax_work <= umax+1e-13: continue
       res=coord_solve(...)
       if res is None: continue
       x,new_aff,newmax,score=res
       oldsum = float(c_work[mlo:mhi+1].sum())  # maybe only if accept; we can compute after? For csum update.
       curr_score = cmax_work + 1e-9*csum_work
       if newmax < cmax_work - 1e-11 or (newmax <= cmax_work + 1e-9 and cmax_work <= best_c + 1e-7 and score < curr_score - 1e-11):
           h_work[p]=x
           c_work[mlo:mhi+1]=new_aff
           cmax_work=newmax
           csum_work += float(new_aff.sum()) - oldsum
           cmax_pref,pref_work,suff_work=refresh_c(c_work)
           cmax_work=cmax_pref
           improved_work=True
           if newmax < cmax_prev - 1e-11: strict_work=True
    end for
    if not improved_work: break
    # project work
    h_eval=project_feasible(h_work,n)
    c_eval=opt_corr(h_eval); cmax_eval=max; csum_eval=max
    # decide commit
    if improved_work:
       if cmax_eval < cmax_feas - 1e-11:
          commit; pass_strict=True
       elif cmax_eval <= cmax_feas + 1e-9 and cmax_eval <= best_c + 1e-7:
          commit tie
       else:
          rollback (h_feas unchanged)
    if not commit: plateau? If no commit break? Could try next pass from same? If improved_work false break. If improved_work true but not commit, maybe break to avoid repeating. Set plateau++ and if plateau>1 break.
    update best if cmax_eval < best_c
    if commit and strict: plateau=0 else plateau++
    if plateau>plateau_limit: break
    # periodic recompute best? already
```

Let's write `generate_shapes` with no closures:
```
def generate_shapes(n,rng):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
    th=0.5*np.pi*x
    out=[]
    h=(np.pi/4.0)*np.sin(th); out.append(project_feasible(h,n))
    for p in (0.7,1.0,1.2,1.5):
       h=np.power(np.clip(np.sin(th),0.0,1.0),p); out.append(project_feasible(h,n))
    h=1.0-np.abs(x-1.0); out.append(project_feasible(h,n))
    for b in (-0.15,-0.05,0.05,0.15):
       raw=np.sin(th)+b*np.sin(3.0*th)
       sm=raw.sum()
       if sm>0: raw=raw*(0.5*n/sm)
       out.append(project_feasible(raw,n))
    for _ in range(6):
       c0=np.pi/4.0; raw=c0*np.sin(th)
       raw += rng.normal(0,0.08)*np.sin(3*th)
       raw += rng.normal(0,0.04)*np.sin(5*th)
       raw += rng.normal(0,0.02)*np.sin(7*th)
       sm=raw.sum()
       if sm>0 and raw.max()>0: raw=raw*(0.5*n/sm)
       out.append(project_feasible(raw,n))
    return out
```
Could include `np.sin(9th)`. Good.

Let's implement `coord_descent` carefully with pass rollback and csum. Code:
```
def coord_descent(h,n,deadline,rng,iters,max_passes,maps,plateau=6):
    h=project_feasible(h.copy(),n)
    c=opt_corr(h)
    cmax=float(c.max()); csum=float(c.sum())
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cmax
    plateau=0
    strict_tol=1e-11
    for pass in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline: break
        h_work=h.copy()
        c_work=c.copy()
        cmax_work=cmax
        csum_work=csum
        cmax_pref,pref,suff=refresh_c(c_work)
        order=rng.permutation(n) if rng is not None else np.arange(n)
        any_update=False; pass_strict=False
        for p in order:
            if time.time() > deadline: break
            entry=maps[p]
            mlo,mhi=entry[0],entry[1]
            umax=float(max(pref[mlo],suff[mhi+1]))
            if not np.isfinite(umax): umax=0.0
            if cmax_work <= umax + 1e-13: continue
            res=coord_solve(c_work,h_work,p,n,cmax_work,umax,iters,entry)
            if res is None: continue
            x,new_aff,newmax,score=res
            oldsum=float(c_work[mlo:mhi+1].sum())
            curr_score=cmax_work + 1e-9*csum_work
            prev_cmax=cmax_work
            accept=False
            if newmax < cmax_work - strict_tol:
                accept=True
            elif newmax <= cmax_work + 1e-9 and cmax_work <= best_c + 1e-7 and newmax <= best_c + 1e-7 and score < curr_score - 1e-11:
                accept=True
            if accept:
                h_work[p]=x
                c_work[mlo:mhi+1]=new_aff
                csum_work += float(new_aff.sum()) - oldsum
                cmax_work,pref,suff=refresh_c(c_work)
                any_update=True
                if cmax_work < prev_cmax - strict_tol:
                    pass_strict=True
        if not any_update:
            break
        h_eval=project_feasible(h_work,n)
        c_eval=opt_corr(h_eval)
        cmax_eval=float(c_eval.max())
        if cmax_eval < best_c - 1e-13:
            best_c=cmax_eval; best_h=h_eval.copy()
        if cmax_eval < cmax - strict_tol or (cmax_eval <= cmax + 1e-9 and cmax_eval <= best_c + 1e-7):
            h=h_eval; c=c_eval; cmax=cmax_eval; csum=float(c.sum())
            if cmax < cmax -? can't use; set
            if pass_strict or cmax_eval < cmax_old - strict_tol: plateau=0
            else: plateau += 1
        else:
            # rollback but maybe if cmax_eval close and score? no
            plateau += 1
            if plateau > plateau:
                break
        if plateau > plateau: break
    # final
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=opt_corr(h); cmax=float(c.max())
    if cmax < best_c: best_c=cmax; best_h=h.copy()
    return best_h,best_c
```

    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    target=0.5*n
    if h.size != n: h=np.resize(h,n)? But not.

       if s > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    x=np.clip(h-0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)

```python
import math
import time

import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size != n:
        h = np.resize(h, n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    lo, hi = -2.0, 2.0
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.clip(h - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    x = np.clip(h - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(x.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0.0:
            for i in range(n):
                step = min(diff, 1.0 - x[i])
                if step > 0.0:
                    x[i] += step
                    diff -= step
                if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
                    break
        else:
            need = -diff
            for i in range(n):
                step = min(need, x[i])
                if step > 0.0:
                    x[i] -= step
                    need -= step
                if need < 1e-15:
                    break
    return x


def compute_c5(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * dx)


def opt_corr(h):
    return np.correlate(1.0 - h, h, mode="full")


def refresh_c(c):
    m = len(c)
    pref = np.empty(m + 1, dtype=float)
    pref[0] = -np.inf
    pref[1:] = np.maximum.accumulate(c)
    suff = np.empty(m + 1, dtype=float)
    suff[m] = -np.inf
    suff[:m] = np.maximum.accumulate(c[::-1])[::-1]
    cmax = float(pref[m]) if m else 0.0
    return cmax, pref, suff


def build_maps(n):
    maps = []
    two_n2 = 2 * n - 2
    for p in range(n):
        mlo = min(p, n - 1 - p)
        mhi = max(p + n - 1, two_n2 - p)
        f_lo = max(mlo, n - 1 - p)
        f_hi = min(mhi, two_n2 - p)
        if f_lo <= f_hi:
            k = f_hi - f_lo + 1
            f_rel = np.arange(f_lo - mlo, f_hi - mlo + 1, dtype=np.int64)
            f_idx = (p + f_lo - (n - 1) + np.arange(k, dtype=np.int64))
        else:
            f_rel = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
            f_idx = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
        s_lo = max(mlo, p)
        s_hi = min(mhi, p + n - 1)
        if s_lo <= s_hi:
            k = s_hi - s_lo + 1
            s_rel = np.arange(s_lo - mlo, s_hi - mlo + 1, dtype=np.int64)
            s_idx = (p + (n - 1) - s_lo - np.arange(k, dtype=np.int64))
        else:
            s_rel = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
            s_idx = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
        maps.append((mlo, mhi, n - 1 - mlo, f_rel, f_idx, s_rel, s_idx))
    return maps


def coord_solve(c, h, p, n, cmax, unaff_max, iters, entry):
    mlo, mhi, diag_rel, f_rel, f_idx, s_rel, s_idx = entry
    h0 = float(h[p])
    length = mhi - mlo + 1
    a = np.zeros(length, dtype=float)
    if f_rel.size:
        a[f_rel] += 1.0 - h[f_idx]
    if s_rel.size:
        a[s_rel] -= h[s_idx]
    a[diag_rel] = 0.0

    c_aff = c[mlo:mhi + 1]
    cdiag = float(c[n - 1])

    if diag_rel == 0:
        a_nd = a[1:]
        c_nd = c_aff[1:]
    elif diag_rel == length - 1:
        a_nd = a[:-1]
        c_nd = c_aff[:-1]
    else:
        a_nd = np.concatenate((a[:diag_rel], a[diag_rel + 1:]))
        c_nd = np.concatenate((c_aff[:diag_rel], c_aff[diag_rel + 1:]))

    pos = a_nd > 1e-14
    neg = a_nd < -1e-14
    has_pos = bool(np.any(pos))
    has_neg = bool(np.any(neg))
    if has_pos:
        ap = a_nd[pos]
        cp = c_nd[pos]
        inv_ap = 1.0 / ap
        const_ap = h0 - cp / ap
    else:
        inv_ap = cp = const_ap = None
    if has_neg:
        an = a_nd[neg]
        cn = c_nd[neg]
        inv_an = 1.0 / an
        const_an = h0 - cn / an
    else:
        inv_an = cn = const_an = None

    lo = max(0.0, unaff_max)
    hi = float(cmax)
    if hi <= lo + 1e-13:
        return None

    span = hi - lo
    if span <= 1e-7:
        its = 12
    elif span <= 1e-4:
        its = 18
    else:
        its = max(14, iters)

    for _ in range(its):
        T = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if has_pos:
            xhi = float(np.min(T * inv_ap + const_ap))
        else:
            xhi = 1.0
        if has_neg:
            xlo = float(np.max(T * inv_an + const_an))
        else:
            xlo = 0.0

        feasible = False
        if xhi >= -1e-13 and xlo <= 1.0 + 1e-13:
            L = 0.0 if xlo < 0.0 else (1.0 if xlo > 1.0 else xlo)
            R = 1.0 if xhi > 1.0 else (0.0 if xhi < 0.0 else xhi)
            if L <= R + 1e-13:
                cq = T - cdiag + h0 - h0 * h0
                if cq < -1e-12:
                    feasible = False
                elif cq >= 0.25 - 1e-14:
                    feasible = True
                else:
                    d = 1.0 - 4.0 * cq
                    if d >= 0.0:
                        rt = math.sqrt(d)
                        r1 = 0.5 * (1.0 - rt)
                        r2 = 0.5 * (1.0 + rt)
                        if L <= r1 + 1e-12 or R >= r2 - 1e-12:
                            feasible = True
        if feasible:
            hi = T
        else:
            lo = T

    T = hi + max(1e-11, 1e-9 * abs(hi))
    if has_pos:
        xhi = float(np.min(T * inv_ap + const_ap))
    else:
        xhi = 1.0
    if has_neg:
        xlo = float(np.max(T * inv_an + const_an))
    else:
        xlo = 0.0
    if xhi < -1e-10 or xlo > 1.0 + 1e-10:
        return None
    L = min(max(xlo, 0.0), 1.0)
    R = min(max(xhi, 0.0), 1.0)
    if L > R + 1e-12:
        return None

    cq = T - cdiag + h0 - h0 * h0
    if cq < -1e-11:
        return None
    intervals = []
    if cq >= 0.25:
        intervals.append((L, R))
    else:
        d = 1.0 - 4.0 * cq
        if d < 0.0:
            return None
        rt = math.sqrt(d)
        r1 = min(max(0.5 * (1.0 - rt), 0.0), 1.0)
        r2 = min(max(0.5 * (1.0 + rt), 0.0), 1.0)
        if L <= r1:
            intervals.append((L, min(R, r1)))
        if R >= r2:
            intervals.append((max(L, r2), R))
    if not intervals:
        intervals.append((L, R))

    cands = []
    for a0, b0 in intervals:
        if b0 < a0:
            continue
        mid = 0.5 * (a0 + b0)
        cands.extend([a0, mid, b0])
    cands.extend([0.0, 1.0, 0.5, h0])
    arr = np.asarray(cands, dtype=float)
    arr = arr[(arr >= -1e-12) & (arr <= 1.0 + 1e-12)]
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1.0)
    arr.sort()
    uniq = []
    last = -1e18
    for v in arr:
        if v - last > 1e-10:
            uniq.append(float(v))
            last = v
    arr = np.asarray(uniq, dtype=float)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None

    if a_nd.size:
        aff = c_nd[None, :] + a_nd[None, :] * (arr[:, None] - h0)
        max_aff = aff.max(axis=1)
        sum_aff = aff.sum(axis=1)
    else:
        max_aff = np.zeros(arr.shape, dtype=float)
        sum_aff = np.zeros(arr.shape, dtype=float)
    diag_vals = cdiag + arr - arr * arr - h0 + h0 * h0
    max_vals = np.maximum(unaff_max, np.maximum(max_aff, diag_vals))
    scores = max_vals + 1e-9 * (sum_aff + diag_vals)
    order = np.lexsort((scores, max_vals))
    idx = int(order[0])
    x = float(arr[idx])

    new_aff = c_aff + a * (x - h0)
    new_aff[diag_rel] = cdiag + x - x * x - h0 + h0 * h0
    newmax = float(max(unaff_max, new_aff.max()))
    score = newmax + 1e-9 * float(new_aff.sum())
    return x, new_aff, newmax, score


def coord_descent(h, n, deadline, rng, iters, max_passes, maps, plateau_limit=6):
    h = project_feasible(h.copy(), n)
    c = opt_corr(h)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    csum = float(c.sum())
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = cmax
    plateau = 0
    strict_tol = 1e-11

    for _pass in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        h_work = h.copy()
        c_work = c.copy()
        cmax_work = cmax
        csum_work = csum
        cmax_work, pref, suff = refresh_c(c_work)
        order = rng.permutation(n) if rng is not None else np.arange(n)
        any_update = False
        pass_strict = False

        for p in order:
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            entry = maps[p]
            mlo, mhi = entry[0], entry[1]
            umax = float(max(pref[mlo], suff[mhi + 1]))
            if not math.isfinite(umax):
                umax = 0.0
            if cmax_work <= umax + 1e-13:
                continue
            res = coord_solve(c_work, h_work, p, n, cmax_work, umax, iters, entry)
            if res is None:
                continue
            x, new_aff, newmax, score = res
            oldsum = float(c_work[mlo:mhi + 1].sum())
            curr_score = cmax_work + 1e-9 * csum_work
            prev = cmax_work
            accept = False
            if newmax < cmax_work - strict_tol:
                accept = True
            elif newmax <= cmax_work + 1e-9 and cmax_work <= best_c + 1e-7 and newmax <= best_c + 1e-7 and score < curr_score - 1e-11:
                accept = True
            if accept:
                h_work[p] = x
                c_work[mlo:mhi + 1] = new_aff
                csum_work += float(new_aff.sum()) - oldsum
                cmax_work, pref, suff = refresh_c(c_work)
                any_update = True
                if cmax_work < prev - strict_tol:
                    pass_strict = True

        if not any_update:
            break

        h_eval = project_feasible(h_work, n)
        c_eval = opt_corr(h_eval)
        cmax_eval = float(c_eval.max())
        csum_eval = float(c_eval.sum())

        if cmax_eval < best_c - 1e-13:
            best_c = cmax_eval
            best_h = h_eval.copy()

        commit = False
        if cmax_eval < cmax - strict_tol:
            commit = True
        elif cmax_eval <= cmax + 1e-9 and cmax_eval <= best_c + 1e-8:
            commit = True

        if commit:
            h, c, cmax, csum = h_eval, c_eval, cmax_eval, csum_eval
            if cmax < cmax_eval:
                pass_strict = True
            if pass_strict:
                plateau = 0
            else:
                plateau += 1
        else:
            break

        if plateau > plateau_limit:
            break

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = opt_corr(h)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax < best_c:
        best_c = cmax
        best_h = h.copy()
    return best_h, best_c


def make_sine_h(coefs, n, terms):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    th = 0.5 * np.pi * x
    raw = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for m in range(terms):
        raw += coefs[m] * np.sin((2 * m + 1) * th)
    sm = float(raw.sum())
    if sm > 1e-12:
        raw *= (0.5 * float(n)) / sm
    return project_feasible(raw, n)


def sine_objective(coefs, n, terms):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    th = 0.5 * np.pi * x
    raw = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for m in range(terms):
        raw += coefs[m] * np.sin((2 * m + 1) * th)
    sm = float(raw.sum())
    if sm <= 1e-12:
        return 1.0 + 10.0 * float(np.maximum(0.0, -raw.min())) + 10.0 * float(np.maximum(0.0, raw.max() - 1.0))
    pen = 10.0 * float(np.maximum(0.0, -raw.min())) + 10.0 * float(np.maximum(0.0, raw.max() - 1.0))
    raw *= (0.5 * float(n)) / sm
    h = project_feasible(raw, n)
    return compute_c5(h, n) + pen


def generate_shapes(n, rng):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    th = 0.5 * np.pi * x
    out = []
    h = (math.pi / 4.0) * np.sin(th)
    out.append(project_feasible(h, n))
    for p in (0.7, 1.0, 1.2, 1.5):
        h = np.power(np.clip(np.sin(th), 0.0, 1.0), p)
        out.append(project_feasible(h, n))
    h = 1.0 - np.abs(x - 1.0)
    out.append(project_feasible(h, n))
    for b in (-0.15, -0.05, 0.05, 0.15):
        raw = np.sin(th) + b * np.sin(3.0 * th)
        sm = float(raw.sum())
        if sm > 1e-12:
            raw *= (0.5 * float(n)) / sm
        out.append(project_feasible(raw, n))
    for _ in range(6):
        raw = (math.pi / 4.0) * np.sin(th)
        raw += rng.normal(0.0, 0.08) * np.sin(3.0 * th)
        raw += rng.normal(0.0, 0.04) * np.sin(5.0 * th)
        raw += rng.normal(0.0, 0.02) * np.sin(7.0 * th)
        sm = float(raw.sum())
        if sm > 1e-12:
            raw *= (0.5 * float(n)) / sm
        out.append(project_feasible(raw, n))
    return out


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 8.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        hpar = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel()
        if hpar.size < 10 or not np.all(np.isfinite(hpar)):
            raise ValueError
        n = int(hpar.size)
        if n > 1000:
            old = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
            n = 1000
            new = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
            hpar = np.interp(new, old, hpar)
        h_init = project_feasible(hpar, n)
    except Exception:
        n = 700
        x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
        h_init = project_feasible((math.pi / 4.0) * np.sin(0.5 * math.pi * x), n)

    best_h = h_init
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)

    if time.time() < deadline - 260.0:
        for hs in generate_shapes(n, rng):
            cs = compute_c5(hs, n)
            if cs < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = hs, cs
        hs = project_feasible(0.5 * (best_h + best_h[::-1]), n)
        cs = compute_c5(hs, n)
        if cs < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hs, cs

    if time.time() < deadline - 260.0:
        starts = [
            [math.pi / 4.0, 0.0, 0.0],
            [math.pi / 4.0, 0.08, -0.03],
            [math.pi / 4.0, -0.06, 0.04],
        ]
        for x0 in starts:
            if time.time() > deadline - 220.0:
                break
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    sine_objective,
                    x0,
                    args=(n, 3),
                    method="Nelder-Mead",
                    options={"maxiter": 70, "xatol": 5e-4, "fatol": 5e-7},
                )
                hc = make_sine_h(res.x, n, 3)
                cc = compute_c5(hc, n)
                if cc < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c = hc, cc
            except Exception:
                pass

    maps = build_maps(n)

    cursor = time.time()
    rem = deadline - cursor
    if rem > 30.0:
        dl = min(deadline, cursor + max(25.0, 0.35 * rem))
        h_new, c_un = coord_descent(best_h, n, dl, rng, 12, 80, maps, plateau_limit=4)
        c_new = c_un * (2.0 / float(n))
        if c_new < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h_new, c_new
        maps = build_maps(n)

    cursor = time.time()
    rem = deadline - cursor
    if rem > 30.0:
        dl = min(deadline, cursor + max(25.0, 0.35 * rem))
        h_new, c_un = coord_descent(best_h, n, dl, rng, 18, 120, maps, plateau_limit=5)
        c_new = c_un * (2.0 / float(n))
        if c_new < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h_new, c_new
        maps = build_maps(n)

    cursor = time.time()
    rem = deadline - cursor
    if rem > 20.0:
        dl = min(deadline, cursor + max(15.0, 0.45 * rem - 60.0))
        h_new, c_un = coord_descent(best_h, n, dl, rng, 26, 220, maps, plateau_limit=8)
        c_new = c_un * (2.0 / float(n))
        if c_new < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h_new, c_new
        maps = build_maps(n)

    if time.time() < deadline - 120.0:
        n_restarts = max(2, int((deadline - time.time() - 90.0) / 55.0))
        for r in range(n_restarts):
            if time.time() > deadline - 70.0:
                break
            sigma = 0.012 + 0.028 * r
            hp = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n), n)
            hp = project_feasible(0.5 * (hp + hp[::-1]), n)
            dl = min(deadline, time.time() + 45.0)
            h_new, c_un = coord_descent(hp, n, dl, rng, 18, 45, maps, plateau_limit=3)
            c_new = c_un * (2.0 / float(n))
            if c_new < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h_new, c_new
        maps = build_maps(n)

    cursor = time.time()
    for n2 in (350, 500, 600, 850, 1000):
        if time.time() > deadline - 160.0:
            break
        if n2 == n or n2 > 1000:
            continue
        x_old = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
        x_new = (np.arange(n2) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n2))
        h2 = project_feasible(np.interp(x_new, x_old, best_h), n2)
        h2s = project_feasible(0.5 * (h2 + h2[::-1]), n2)
        if compute_c5(h2s, n2) < compute_c5(h2, n2):
            h2 = h2s
        maps2 = build_maps(n2)
        dl = min(deadline, time.time() + 80.0)
        h2, c_un2 = coord_descent(h2, n2, dl, rng, 16, 55, maps2, plateau_limit=4)
        c2 = c_un2 * (2.0 / float(n2))
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, n = h2, c2, n2
            maps = maps2

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(n)
```
